# AUDIT

> Open audit findings. `/iterate` reads the Pending section
> and ships the highest-scoring item per tick. Score formula:
> `impact × ease / 10`, then apply the bias multiplier from
> the header (if present) for the matching category.
>
> Categories: `bug`, `perf`, `a11y`, `seo`, `content-gaps`,
> `data`, `docs`, `mod`, `spoiler`, `other`.

- [ ] [LOW] Themed-list `description` field is over the ~155-160 char SERP-truncation budget on roughly 155 of the ~181 themes in the catalog — discovered while resolving CRITIQUE pass-105/110/112/104's MED+LOW findings (5 flagged instances), which framed this as a narrow, occasional defect. A full-catalog scan (`content/themes/*.md`, grepping the `description` frontmatter field) found the opposite: near-universal violation, many themes running 220-280 chars, nearly double the budget. The CRITIQUE finding's own suggested fix — "add a `content:check` lint rule that fails the build when `description` exceeds ~160 chars" — is NOT applied: shipping it strict today would red the build across most of the catalog, and shipping it lax/warn-only would just accumulate silent debt the way the count-tail and card-tagline-gap invariants were designed specifically to avoid (see `collectThemeDescriptionCountTailIssues` / `collectCardTaglineGapIssues` in `scripts/content-check.ts` — both shipped strict at floor 0 because their rewrite drained every offender in the same tick, which isn't feasible here at ~155 files). This needs an oversight decision between two paths: **(a)** a dedicated mass-trim content pass across ~155 files (large, low-risk, mechanical — likely several ticks' worth of `/ship-content` or a dedicated `/expand`-tracked phase), or **(b)** relaxing the ~155-160 char guidance itself, since near-universal violation across independently-authored lists suggests the budget may be miscalibrated for this site's longer editorial voice rather than every list being wrong. Recommend (b) get a first look — check what actual Google SERP behavior looks like today (the 155-160 guidance is a long-standing rule of thumb, not a hard platform limit) before committing to a 155-file rewrite. Not urgent (SEO-only, no visible-page or spoiler impact, no user-facing breakage) — parking for the next human-run `/oversight` session rather than auto-scoring for pickup. (category: content-gaps, source: self, score: 1.5 — impact 5 × ease 3, deliberately low to keep it out of automatic dispatch pending the oversight call) — filed 2026-08-11 via cloud march tick while resolving CRITIQUE pass-105/110/112/104; see `plan/CRITIQUE.md` resolution notes on both closed rows for the five directly-fixed instances.

Both `season-page.spec.ts:172` and `smoke-mobile.spec.ts:25` fail identically on all four URLs — four different shows, four different season slugs, same 375px overflow signature — which rules out a per-content string fix and confirms the shared-component-CSS branch of the original diagnosis. The same run also missed the 75-minute crawl ceiling (only 9,392/9,822 tests complete, 95.6%) — the retries this defect triggers (2 retries × 8 failing specs = 16 extra test executions per night, and growing) are plausibly a compounding contributor to that separate timeout erosion, so fixing this bug may relax both symptoms at once. issue #568's thread has degraded to nightly auto-"Recurred" comments with no re-triage since the original 07-13 comment — nobody has looked at the trace files yet. Highest-scoring non-standing AUDIT row for 7 consecutive digest windows; see `plan/PHASE_CANDIDATES.md` candidate #33 for the structural reason `/iterate` hasn't reached it. **Update (digest 2026-07-20):** the e2e-full breadth crawl's gone red an 8th consecutive night on this defect (07-13 through 07-19's run, read tonight; run 29707752367) but the affected-URL count held flat at **4** for the first time — no growth since 07-19's addition of `love-island-us/season/fiji-2026`. The 75-minute crawl step ran to its literal wall clock again (23:23:17→00:38:29, exactly 75m12s) and was cut mid-suite at test 9411/9927 (94.8% complete, consistent with the prior two nights' ~95% ceiling — not eroding further, not recovering either). New signal worth acting on: `plan/CRITIQUE.md` pass 94 (2026-07-16, still the latest pass, 4 days stale) independently diagnosed the exact same URL (`/shows/the-real-world/season/go-big-or-go-home`) via the cloud walker and already narrowed the culprit to one of three specific candidates — the long `location`/`filming_caption` string, the forced `<br/>` in `display_title: "Go Big<br/>or Go Home"`, or `AdjacentSeasons.tsx`/`.season-related` rendering (`src/styles/screens.css:1102-1114`) — and already ruled out the `@media (max-width: 560px)` stacking rule as the cause. Two independent detection paths (nightly breadth crawl + critique cloud walker) have now converged on the same URL with the same overflow signature; pass 94's narrower diagnosis gives `/iterate` (once it can reach this row) a much shorter path to a fix than a cold repro would need. — RESOLVED 4557353: neither of the flagged candidates (AdjacentSeasons, display_title `<br/>`, location/filming_caption) was the actual cause — empirical Playwright measurement (build+serve, per-element getBoundingClientRect scan at 375px) pinned two distinct CSS bugs converging on the same symptom: `.stat-val` lacked `overflow-wrap: break-word` (an unbreakable slash-joined `format_summary` compound word overflowed the 2-column mobile stats grid — affects american-ninja-warrior/the-runoffs and the-real-world/go-big-or-go-home) and `.ep-foot` carried unconditional `white-space: nowrap` (a long `episode_heat_caption` couldn't shrink in the mobile flex episode strip — affects the-challenge/vets-and-new-threats and love-island-us/fiji-2026). Fixed both in `src/styles/screens.css`, `.ep-foot` scoped to the existing 560px breakpoint so desktop is untouched. All four affected URLs plus a control page now measure `scrollWidth === clientWidth` at 375px. Verify gate green: 195 test files / 3545 unit tests, build (1407 pages), 4560 e2e (27.3m). Closes #568.
- [x] [LOW] `skills/ship-content.md` Step 2 (GitHub mirror) always calls `gh issue create` fresh instead of find-or-reuse — unlike the phase mirror (`scripts/loop-issue.mjs phase-open`), which is idempotent by title-prefix. When a ship-content tick files the mirror issue and then fails/times out before its own close step, the issue is permanently orphaned: the next successful tick for the same show creates a brand-new issue rather than reusing the old number, and the orphan never gets a `Closes #N` trailer or close-comment. Surfaced this tick via /triage: issue #577 (90 Day Fiancé) shipped successfully in commit `1bbfde4` but was never closed — the shipping tick's own `$N` didn't reference it; issue #580 (Married at First Sight Australia) was opened by a tick that filed the mirror and then never shipped the content at all (CADENCE still reads gap 12/13). Both closed manually this tick with explanatory comments. Fix: give `loop-issue.mjs` a `content-open`-style find-or-reuse subcommand (mirroring `phase-open`'s prefix-match logic, keyed on show slug in the title) so ship-content reuses an existing open mirror instead of creating a duplicate. (category: bug, source: triage, score: 3.0 — impact 5 × ease 6) — filed 2026-07-14 via /triage — issues: #577, #580 — RESOLVED 87bad709: added `content-open` subcommand to `scripts/loop-issue.mjs` mirroring `phase-open`'s find-or-reuse shape, keyed on a suffix match (` — <show-or-theme-name>`) rather than prefix since the title's unit-type segment varies across Rules while the trailing display name is the stable identity across a show's multi-tick drain. Reuses an OPEN mirror by number, reopens+comments on a CLOSED one, only creates fresh when no match exists. Wired into `skills/ship-content.md` Step 2. New tests in `scripts/__tests__/loop-issue.test.mjs` (`contentTitleSuffix`, `isContentMatch`, `buildContentResumeCommentBody`, `LABEL_PALETTE['loop:content']`). Full verify gate green (3551 vitest + 145 node:test, build, 4856/4856 e2e). Phase 46's shipping commit `9ed40d5` ("feat: colocated-test gate walks src/app/** — phase 46") contains `Closes #46` — the *phase number*, not the mirror issue number — which resolves to an unrelated, already-closed 2026-05-14 issue about a Vercel config bug (confirmed via `gh issue view 46`), leaving the actual mirror, issue #405, open. Both #400 and #405 remain open as of this digest (2026-07-20). Checked all 30 closed phase-mirror issues (Phase 1 through Phase 45, excluding 44/46) via `gh issue list --state closed --search "in:title Phase"` — every one shows `stateReason: COMPLETED`, so this looks like an isolated two-phase regression, not a systemic pattern, but the mechanism (confusing the phase ordinal with the mirror issue number, or omitting the trailer outright) could recur on any future phase and should be checked at `/ship-a-phase`'s close step rather than caught after the fact. Distinct from the already-tracked row above (that one is about `/ship-content`'s mirror lacking find-or-reuse; this is about `/ship-a-phase`'s mirror having a wrong/missing close trailer). Fix: manually close #400 and #405 with explanatory comments (not done by this digest — notes-only scope), then have `/ship-a-phase` verify its own `Closes #N` trailer resolves to an open issue whose title matches the phase before finalizing the commit. - [ ] [LOW] `YEAR_TENURE_RE` in `scripts/content-check.ts` only matches spelled-out tens (twenty–ninety) — teen-number tenure phrases ("fourteen-year", "twelve years in") are structurally invisible to the editorial-tenure-honesty invariant. Surfaced by CRITIQUE pass-80's The Voice finding (closed this tick, commit 0c917a9 / issue #504), whose suggested fix included "widen `YEAR_TENURE_RE` to also match teen numbers" — deferred rather than shipped alongside the content fix. Corpus scan (grep across `content/shows/*.md`, `*/seasons/*.md`, `*/canon.md`) found the widening is NOT a simple regex edit: at least 10 existing teen-number tenure phrases are historical facts anchored to a specific past season's air date, not live claims against "today" — e.g. `amazing-race/seasons/11-all-stars.md` "first ten years back on the starting line", `naked-and-afraid/seasons/18-the-long-haul.md` "twelve years in, eighteen seasons deep", `the-voice/seasons/20-the-decade.md` "Ten years... marked ten years of spinning chairs", `bachelorette.md`/`canon.md` "first Black lead in fifteen years", `big-brother/canon.md` "returns after fourteen years", `masterchef-australia/canon.md` "Ten years in — Gordon Ramsay's debut". Widening the regex today would make every one of these fail the strict `YEAR_TENURE_STRICT` gate on the next tick after filing (each show's live `yearsSinceEst(today)` has already moved past the historical count), since `TENURE_ANCHOR_ALLOWLIST` only exempts entries by (show, canon-entry-title) pair — season-level eyebrow/lede/pull sources have no `entryTitle` and can't be allowlisted through that path today. Real fix needs one of: (a) build ~10 allowlist-equivalent entries for season-level sources (requires extending `TenureAnchor`/`isAnchorAllowed` to accept a season-file scope, not just canon `entryTitle`), or (b) exempt `status: ended` shows from `collectYearTenureIssues` entirely (a show's finished tenure is a frozen historical fact, not a value that should keep tracking "today" — same principle just applied to The Voice's specific fix) and separately allowlist the handful of remaining teen-number historical mentions on still-airing shows. Needs a scoped one-tick fix of its own, not a bolt-on to a content edit. (category: engineering, source: self, score: 2.7 — impact 3 × ease 9) — filed 2026-07-08 during iterate pass closing CRITIQUE pass-80's The Voice finding — **re-scoped (2026-08-12, cloud march tick):** re-verified against the current catalog (5+ weeks of drain growth since filing) by simulating the widened regex directly against `collectYearTenureIssues()`. Findings that change the risk profile: **(1) scope grew, not shrank** — 22 issues across 8 shows today, not ~10. **(2) the `status: ended` exemption (option b) only clears 6 of those 22** — `so-you-think-you-can-dance` and `the-real-world` are the only two of the eight affected shows actually `status: ended`; the other six (`amazing-race`, `bachelorette`, `big-brother`, `dancing-with-the-stars`, `naked-and-afraid`, `rhoc`) are `airing`, and `the-voice` is `hiatus` — none of those seven are covered by an ended-show exemption. **(3) `the-voice` is a hard blocker, not just another anchor case** — its three flagged instances (`the-voice.md` tagline, `29-the-finale.md` pull, `canon.md` #28 tag, all "fourteen years") ARE the live factual-corruption this file already tracks at the standing HIGH row above (score 4.8, mirrored as issue #762): that show's real tenure claim is disputed/fabricated, not a legitimate milestone. Widening the regex would force `YEAR_TENURE_STRICT` to fail the build on `the-voice` immediately — anchoring the phrase to make it pass would launder the fabrication through the allowlist (the exact move issue #762 already warns against — "do not attempt to fact-correct it... until the full remediation lands"). Any regex-widening commit MUST ship an explicit `the-voice` skip in `collectYearTenureIssues` (not an anchor) referencing #762, or it cannot land without either breaking the build or compounding the corruption. **(4) a semantic false-positive class the original filing didn't catch**: the regex/anchor mechanism can't distinguish "the show itself is N years old" from an unrelated "N years" fact about a person or element. `rhoc/seasons/19-the-resurfacing.md` + `canon.md` #17's "Gretchen Rossi returns after twelve years away" (3-4 instances) is about a *cast member's* absence, not the show's tenure (RHOC est. 2006, 12 ≠ 20) — using the show-tenure anchor mechanism to suppress it would be a category error, since the anchor's own doc comment scopes it to genuine milestone-anniversary claims only. This phrase needs a rephrase (drop the literal, per the check's own suggested remedy) or a differently-scoped exemption, not a tenure anchor. **(5) the original filing's own three cited examples don't even match its proposed fix**: `amazing-race/seasons/11-all-stars.md` ("first ten years"), `the-voice/seasons/20-the-decade.md` ("ten years... Ten years in"), and `masterchef-australia/canon.md` ("Ten years in") all use the bare word "ten," which sits between the tens-list (twenty–ninety) and the teens-list (eleven–nineteen) — widening to teens per the filed proposal would NOT catch any of the filer's own examples; a third regex branch for bare "ten" is a separate gap. **Net: this is genuinely NOT a scoped one-tick fix** — a correct, honest implementation needs the `the-voice` skip (item 3), a `status:ended` exemption (clears 2 shows), a rephrase (not anchor) for the RHOC person-absence false-positive (item 4), a bare-"ten" regex branch (item 5), and verified season-scoped anchors for the remaining ~5 genuine airing-show milestone facts (`amazing-race` S19, `bachelorette` S13, `big-brother` #3 entry, `dancing-with-the-stars` S31's vague "fifteen-plus years" hedge, `naked-and-afraid` S18) — each requires extending `TenureAnchor`/`isAnchorAllowed` to match on season number (canon entries already carry `entry.season`; season-file sources need a `seasonNumber` added to the `Source` type) since none of these are canon-entry-scoped. No code changed this tick (investigated via a local, reverted simulation only — `git diff scripts/content-check.ts` confirmed clean before this note was written). Recommend a dedicated `/expand`-tracked phase or oversight-reviewed tick given the item-3/item-4 correctness risk, rather than a cloud tick attempting this cold. Score unchanged at 2.7 (still LOW, still below the 3.0 auto-dispatch floor) — this note exists so a future pick doesn't re-derive the same investigation or, worse, ship the naive version and either red the build on `the-voice` or quietly anchor a fabricated claim.

     (canon completeness) auto-files per-show season rows that drain

- [x] [LOW] Pin Supabase CLI version in e2e-full workflow to prevent transient Gateway Timeout failures — `.github/workflows/e2e-full.yml`'s `supabase/setup-cli@v1` step uses `version: latest`, which queries the Supabase release API at runtime; when that API is briefly unavailable the step fails before any e2e test runs (hit 2026-06-14 original, recurred 2026-07-04 05:23). Fix: pin a specific Supabase CLI version in the workflow to remove the runtime release-resolution fetch. (category: bug, source: triage, score: 4.8 — impact 6 × ease 8) — filed 2026-06-14 as `### [user-issue #416]` — reformatted 2026-07-06 (digest) into the standard bullet shape this section's own format comment specifies; the heading form doesn't match `/iterate`'s `- [ ] [SEV] ...` scan pattern, which is the likely reason this sat unshipped for 3+ expand passes (41/42/43/44 each independently noted it "unchanged," pass 44 flagging it explicitly as "not a clustering signal") despite scoring above every ease/impact threshold that ships lower-scored rows same-tick — issue: #416 — BLOCKED FROM CLOUD 2026-07-06: attempted the pin (`version: latest` → a fixed version) across `e2e-full.yml`, `migrate.yml`, `march.yml`; `git push` was rejected by GitHub with "refusing to allow a GitHub App to create or update workflow .github/workflows/e2e-full.yml without workflows permission" — the cloud loop's GH App token structurally lacks the `workflows` OAuth scope. Reverted the commit (never pushed elsewhere, safe to discard). This fix cannot ship from any cloud `/march` tick as currently scoped — needs a local/`/oversight` session with a token that carries the `workflows` scope. Do not re-attempt from cloud until that scope is granted. — **RESOLVED 2aecc02 (oversight 2026-07-12, local session):** pinned `version: 2.106.0` (proven green in this pipeline on the #402 resolution run) across `e2e-full.yml`, `march.yml`, `migrate.yml`. Closes #416/#480.

- [x] [HIGH] [user-issue #402] Cloud verify-gate e2e leg is structurally red because the Supabase `service_role` has only `TRUNCATE / REFERENCES / TRIGGER` on every phase-11/12/13 `public.*` table (`users`, `sessions`, `votes`, `comments`, `flags`, `mod_actions`, `ai_decisions`, `rank_snapshots`) — no `SELECT / INSERT / UPDATE / DELETE`. Every authed-write spec (`comment-backend`, `comment-read`, `community-page`, `ranking-api`, `user-profile`, `vote-state-pill`) trips on `POST /api/vote` or `POST /api/comment` returning 500 with `{"ok":false,"error":"auth_resolve_failed","detail":"upsertUser: permission denied for table users"}`. RLS bypass on `service_role` doesn't override missing GRANTs at the postgres layer. Root cause: migrations run as the `postgres` role, whose `pg_default_acl` in schema `public` does NOT include grants to `anon / authenticated / service_role` — only objects created by `supabase_admin` get the auto-grants the Supabase API roles need. Cloud loop has been hitting this since ~2026-06-11 10:06Z; supabase CLI 2.106.0 in this run. (category: data, source: triage, score: 7.2 — impact 9 × ease 8) — e772dfc — issue: #402 — RESOLVED this commit: took fix shape (a) — added `supabase/migrations/20260611000001_grant_api_roles.sql` that explicitly grants `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE` on the eight in-scope tables (`users`, `sessions`, `votes`, `comments`, `flags`, `ai_decisions`, `mod_actions`, `rank_snapshots`) to `anon, authenticated, service_role`. The migration matches the standard Supabase pattern for tables created by `supabase_admin`; the existing RLS policies in migrations 000001/000003/000004/000007/000008/000009/000013/000015 continue to gate row-level access for anon and authenticated, and service_role bypasses RLS as before — GRANTs are the postgres ACL layer, RLS is the row filter, both must permit. Belt-and-braces: also (b) `grant usage, select on all sequences in schema public` (no current bigserial columns; defends future ones), and (c) `alter default privileges for role postgres in schema public grant ... on tables` + `... on sequences` so any future `create table` migration applied by `postgres` inherits the GRANTs automatically and a regression cannot silently re-ship locked tables. Migration is idempotent on `db reset`. Proof of fix: the same red e2e specs went green — leg 3 ran 1362 passed (was 13 failed before the migration on this very tick). Verify gate green all three legs: leg1 tokens + typecheck + no-raw-img + colocation + 193 unit files / 2684 tests + script + content:check (13 shows / 298 seasons / 13 canons / 12 themes / 3 legal docs); leg2 build; leg3 1362 e2e (incl. the 13 previously-failing authed-write specs). Closes #402.

- [x] [HIGH] `pnpm verify` is structurally broken on every Windows checkout — two independent environment bugs, both verified 2026-06-11 by /oversight on a clean main working tree and both invisible to the Linux-only cloud loop + Vercel: (1) the phase-42 colocation gate exits 1 with `found 308 testless source module(s)` (every module flagged, including the `__tests__/*.test.*` files themselves) because `scripts/check-test-colocation.mjs` walks with `node:path` `join` — backslash separators on win32 — but filters test files with the POSIX literal `'/__tests__/'` (line 57) and the pure lib `scripts/lib/check-test-colocation.mjs` splits/joins on `'/'` in `expectedTestPaths`/`resolveCandidates`; fix by normalizing walked paths to forward slashes at the CLI boundary before they reach the lib, and pin a regression test feeding backslash paths; (2) `src/content/__tests__/hvv-take-shape-opener-echo.test.ts` fails 3/7 on Windows (`expected '' to match …` — its TAKE/SHAPE section extraction returns empty) on both vitest 3.2.4 and 3.2.6, the CRLF-checkout class: the extraction regex anchors on `\n` while a default `core.autocrlf` checkout delivers CRLF in `content/shows/survivor/seasons/20-heroes-vs-villains.md`; normalize `\r\n` to `\n` before matching (the sibling echo tests pass on Windows — mirror their approach). Until both land, the local verify gate cannot go green on the user's machine, which silently blocks every local shipping skill. (category: bug, source: self, score: 6.3) — 98074f3 — issue: #407 — RESOLVED 99415fd: took the row's full path. **(1)** Added `toPosix(p)` helper to `scripts/lib/check-test-colocation.mjs` (exported); the CLI wrapper applies it at every FS boundary — `process.cwd()` (the ROOT prefix), every path produced by `path.join` in `walk()`, and the joined root passed to `walk()` for each `ROOTS` entry. `relative()` simplifies to a single-form prefix strip. The lib remains POSIX-only — paths reach it already in `/`-separated form, so `expectedTestPaths` / `resolveCandidates` (which split/join on `'/'`) work uniformly across platforms. **(2)** `src/content/__tests__/hvv-take-shape-opener-echo.test.ts` gained a `readRaw()` helper that normalizes `\r\n` → `\n` once before either match runs; `readBody()` + `readPull()` thread through it. The sibling `best-finales-hvv-title-canon-echo.test.ts` precedent (uses `\r?\n` and passes on Windows) is named in a header comment so the rationale survives future authoring. **Regression pin**: four new `node:test` cases in `scripts/__tests__/check-test-colocation.test.mjs` — `toPosix` converts backslashes; `toPosix` is a no-op on POSIX paths; `toPosix` normalizes mixed-separator paths; and the load-bearing behavioral assertion that, after `toPosix`, the CLI's `/__tests__/` filter correctly distinguishes a Windows test path (`C:\foo\src\components\__tests__\X.test.tsx`) from a Windows source path (`C:\foo\src\components\X.tsx`). A future change that drops the normalization trips at unit time, not the next Windows checkout. Verify gate green on Linux (cloud): leg1 tokens + typecheck + no-raw-img + colocation + 193 unit files / 2697 tests + 88 script tests + content:check (13 shows / 298 seasons / 13 canons / 12 themes / 3 legal docs); leg2 build; leg3 1362 e2e. Closes #407.

- [x] [MED] `/` (the home page) emits zero JSON-LD — the only top-level public surface in the product without machine-readable structured data, the bare spot a `WebSite`-type signal is meant to fill. Every other page family emits its own type-specific JSON-LD via `jsonLdScriptProps`: show pages emit `CollectionPage` + `ItemList` + `BreadcrumbList` (`src/app/shows/[show]/page.tsx`), season pages emit `Article` + `BreadcrumbList` (`src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/page.tsx`), `/shows` emits `ItemList`, `/themes` + `/themes/[theme]` emit `CollectionPage`/`ItemList`, `/about` emits `FAQPage`, `/u/[handle]` emits `ProfilePage`. `src/app/(default)/page.tsx:36-45` defines `generateMetadata` (title, description, canonical, OG, RSS discovery) but the render function emits no `<script type="application/ld+json">` at all — `grep -rn '@type.*WebSite\\|@type.*Organization' src/ content/` returns zero matches. The brand's root surface is the canonical place to declare the publisher in a way crawlers can read; absent that, Google has to infer the publisher from OG tags alone (less reliable, weaker for brand panels in SERPs). schema.org's `WebSite` is the documented type for this — declared once on the home page, name + description + url. Do **not** include a `SearchAction` / `potentialAction.SearchAction` block — `/search` is not a real route in the product (the header's `SearchTrigger` replaced a legacy `/search` link, see `src/components/search/SearchTrigger.tsx:5`); declaring a SearchAction whose `urlTemplate` 404s would mislead crawlers and is worse than emitting nothing. Fix: (a) extend `buildJsonLd` in `src/lib/seo.ts` with a new `WebSite` arm (`name: siteConfig.name`, `description`, `url: canonicalUrl('/')` — keep the existing tagged-union shape; no SearchAction); (b) call it from `src/app/(default)/page.tsx`, render `<script {...jsonLdScriptProps({ id: 'ld-website', data: ld })} />` inside the page tree, mirroring `/about`'s FAQ pattern; (c) extend `src/lib/__tests__/seo.test.ts` (`buildJsonLd` cases) with a `WebSite` case pinning `@type`/`name`/`description`/`url`; (d) extend `src/app/(default)/__tests__/page.test.tsx` with a "home emits a WebSite JSON-LD script on render" case asserting the parsed `@type === 'WebSite'`, the `url` is the home canonical, and there is no `potentialAction` key (negative pin against accidentally adding a SearchAction). No URL/schema/route change. (category: a11y, source: self, score: 4.8 — impact 6 × ease 8) — issue: #229 — RESOLVED this commit: the profile family **cannot** join the flat anon matrix in `a11y.spec.ts` — its handle is discovered at runtime (it is never in env: the cloud e2e leg sets `E2E_USER_EMAIL`/`PASSWORD` but not `E2E_USER_HANDLE`, and `user-profile.spec.ts` reads the `/u/<handle>` href off the authed header), and the `users` row only exists after the e2e user acts (the webServer chain runs `supabase db reset --no-seed`). So the axe scan was added where that authed setup already lives: a new dedicated test `'public profile view is a11y-clean (WCAG 2.1 AA critical/serious)'` in `apps/e2e/tests/user-profile.spec.ts`, inside the existing `test.use({ storageState })` describe block (so it self-skips when the cookie cache is empty, runs in cloud where the chain mints the cookie). It mirrors the activity test's setup — authed populate via the module-level `castVote(page, 1)` helper (→ `users` row upserted → profile resolves + `ProfileStats` renders) + header handle discovery — then `context.clearCookies()` and `runA11yScan({ page, url: profilePath })`, the SAME shared helper (identical `wcag2a/aa` + `wcag21a/aa` tags, `color-contrast` disabled, critical/serious-only filter) the matrix uses, so coverage is uniform. The profile components were verified a11y-clean on read — `ProfileHeader` (header→h1→p), `ProfileStats` (valid `dl`>`div`>`dt`/`dd` grouping), `ProfileComments` (`ul`/`li`, every `<Link>` carries discernible text), `ProfileEmpty` (sparse divs + labeled CTA) — so this lands as a passing regression guard, not a fix-then-pass. `a11y.spec.ts` gains a header comment cross-referencing where the profile scan lives so the matrix file still answers "where's the a11y coverage". `/u/[handle]` is not a new URL (shipped phase 10, drained phase 38) so no canonical-urls/page-reads/unit-test row is owed — purely an axe-coverage extension on an existing contracted family.

(2) **`generateMetadata` doc-present path** (`:9-17`) — title from `doc.title`, description `doc.description ?? 'Terms of Service.'`, canonical `/terms` via real `buildMetadata`; a regression in the slug or the `??` fallback drifts the SEO title/snippet. (3) **`generateMetadata` doc-absent fallback** (`:11`) — when `getLegalDoc('terms')` returns null (the `content/legal/terms.md` file renamed/removed) the route still returns a valid Metadata (`title: 'Terms'`, `description: 'Terms of Service.'`, `/terms`) rather than crashing the build; the walk only ever sees the doc present. (4) **default export** (`:19-27`) — doc present → renders `<article>` wrapping `<Prose source={doc.body_md} />`; doc absent → `notFound()`. The notFound branch is entirely dark to e2e (the file is always present in the walk). Pinning `getLegalDoc('terms')` exactly also guards the sibling copy-paste hazard described above. - [x] [MED] `src/app/(default)/u/[handle]/page.tsx` (the phase-38 public reader profile) ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` page after the #210 `/mod` queue pin. The phase-42 colocated-coverage gate (`scripts/check-test-colocation.mjs`) walks `src/components/**`, `src/lib/**`, `src/content/**` but **not** `src/app/**`, so app-route pages are drained reactively by `/iterate`. The profile page resolves a handle → `users` row via the `profile_activity` Supabase RPC and carries five contracts none of which a hermetic e2e walk pins from the outside (it only ever walks the e2e user's own populated page, as a single viewer): (1) **`notFound()` only on a genuinely-unknown handle** (`src/app/(default)/u/[handle]/page.tsx:108`) — a real member who isn't the signed-in viewer must still render for any visitor (the phase-38 "404 only on unknown, not 'not me'" rule); a regression that 404'd non-self profiles would hide every member's page from everyone but themselves. (2) **noIndex discipline** (`generateMetadata`, `:96`) — empty profiles stay `noIndex` (no thin-content page in the index) while populated ones are indexable; the walk reads rendered pages, not the `Metadata` object. (3) **self-view detection** (`:118`) — the signed-in viewer's derived handle (via the real `headerUserFromSession`) is matched against the profile handle to gate the empty-state CTA, and ONLY then is `getFeaturedShow()/getCanon()` reached; e2e sees one viewer, not the cross-viewer branch. (4) **PII guard** (`publicDisplayName`, `:63`) — an email-shaped `display_name` (Auth0 sets `name` to the email on magic-link sign-in) is dropped to null before it reaches the public byline + the JSON-LD `name`; a regression would leak a real address to anonymous visitors. (5) **JSON-LD only when populated** (`:154-156`) — a thin empty profile emits no `ProfilePage` structured data. - [x] [MED] `src/app/(default)/mod/page.tsx` (the RBAC-gated moderation queue, phase 13) ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` page-level route after the #211 root-layout + #212 per-show-layout pins drained the two layout files ahead of it. The phase-42 colocated-coverage gate (`scripts/check-test-colocation.mjs`) walks `src/components/**`, `src/lib/**`, `src/content/**` but **not** `src/app/**`, so app-route pages are drained reactively by `/iterate`. `/mod` carries a **P0-protective leak guard** plus a three-branch auth gate, none of which a hermetic e2e walk pins from the outside (the walk needs a `mod`-role session to reach the queue and only ever exercises the gate at the network level): (1) **segment config** (`runtime = 'nodejs'` + `dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`) — the queue ordering changes on every action; static/ISR would serve a stale or cross-viewer queue. (2) **`generateMetadata` `noIndex: true`** — load-bearing because the queue renders pending/hidden comment BODIES (potential spoilers); indexing /mod would surface unreviewed content in SERP snippets. (3) **three-branch gate** — no session → signed-out; signed-in-no-`mod:read` → not-authorized; `mod:read` → queue. (4) **the P0**: `getModQueue()` (pending + hidden comment bodies) must fire ONLY on the verified-mod branch — a regression hoisting it above the gate or inverting `!isMod` would drain the queue for anon/non-mod viewers, an outright spoiler/moderation leak. (5) **item-count pluralization**. - [x] [MED] `src/app/shows/[show]/layout.tsx` (the per-show SEGMENT layout) ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` file after the #211 root-layout pin. The phase-42 colocated-coverage gate (`scripts/check-test-colocation.mjs`) walks `src/components/**`, `src/lib/**`, `src/content/**` but **not** `src/app/**`, so app-route layouts are drained reactively by `/iterate`. This is the load-bearing chrome wrapper every show home AND every season page inherits — the highest-traffic page family (13 shows × ~298 season slugs). None of its contracts are pinned by a hermetic e2e walk (the walk reads the rendered page, not the layout's prop construction): (1) **`notFound()` branch** (`src/app/shows/[show]/layout.tsx:19-20`) — `getShow(params.show)` → `notFound()` on an unknown show; a fall-through with `show: null` would crash the downstream `show.palette` read, but e2e only ever walks known shows. (2) **Per-show palette injection** (`src/app/shows/[show]/layout.tsx:25`) — `<ShowPaletteScope show={show.slug} palette={show.palette} asSegment>` is the whole "color does the visual work" mechanism (CLAUDE.md visual law); `asSegment` makes it a layout-level scope covering chrome + every child route rather than a page-level one; e2e sees the painted page, not which prop drove the paint, so a regression dropping `asSegment` or mis-threading the palette (e.g. passing `params.show` instead of `show.slug`) is dark to it. (3) **`tinted` Header + Footer** (`src/app/shows/[show]/layout.tsx:27,31`) — the prop that takes the chrome onto the show palette rather than the default paper; a regression dropping it would render default-paper chrome on a tinted show page. (4) **SkipToMain first** (`src/app/shows/[show]/layout.tsx:26`) — the skip link must be the first focusable element (an a11y ordering the axe walk checks for presence but not authored position). (5) **`<main id="main" className="flex-1">`** (`src/app/shows/[show]/layout.tsx:28`) — the skip-link target + flex shell, plus the SearchHost fed from `getSearchIndex()`. - [x] [MED] `src/app/layout.tsx` (the ROOT layout) ships without a colocated test — the next highest-impact testless `src/app/` file after the #131–#206 drain pinned every `src/app/api/` route handler, `src/middleware.ts`, `src/app/sitemap.ts`, `src/app/robots.ts`, both feed routes, every `*/opengraph-image.tsx` variant + the root `src/app/opengraph-image.tsx`, the phase-33 `permanentRedirect` pair (#180 / #193), and the `/sign-in` magic-link page (#206). The root layout is inherited by **every** route on tiered.tv — default chrome routes, show + season tinted routes, internal demos — and by every SEO/social surface. None of its contracts are pinned by a hermetic e2e walk (the walk reads rendered pages, not the `Metadata`-object construction nor the inline bootstrap-script string): (1) **`metadata` export** (`src/app/layout.tsx:7-39`) — `metadataBase = new URL(siteConfig.baseUrl)` (every relative canonical/OG URL resolves against it; a regression hardcoding a different base or dropping the URL wrapper breaks all of them), `title.default` (the full brand promise) + `title.template` (`'%s — tiered.tv'`, applied to every child route's `<title>` — a regression dropping it strips the brand suffix site-wide), `description` (spoiler-free + the two-rankings frame), `applicationName`, the global RSS `application/rss+xml` alternate at `/feed.xml` (feed-reader auto-discovery, dark to e2e — the walk fetches `/feed.xml` directly, never greps the `<head>` alternate), the favicon set SVG-first with the `.ico` universal fallback + apple-touch-icon (order is load-bearing for which asset a browser picks). (2) **`viewport` export** (`src/app/layout.tsx:41-45`) — `themeColor = '#0E0B08'` (brand paper, the mobile browser-chrome tint), `width = 'device-width'`, `initialScale = 1`. (3) **No-flicker theme bootstrap** (`src/app/layout.tsx:47,52-54`) — a synchronous IIFE reading `localStorage.getItem('tiered_theme')`, gating on `'light'||'dark'`, applying `documentElement.dataset.theme`, wrapped in `try/catch` so a blocked localStorage (private mode) can't throw before first paint. Never fires during the e2e walk (no theme set), so the branch is entirely dark to it; a regression dropping the gate (applies any junk value, breaking the CSS theme cascade) or the try/catch (throws + blocks paint in private mode) would ship unnoticed. (4) **`<html>`/`<body>` shell** (`src/app/layout.tsx:50-62`) — `<html lang="en" suppressHydrationWarning>` (the suppress flag is required because the bootstrap mutates `dataset.theme` before hydration), the `<body className="flex min-h-dvh flex-col">` sticky-footer shell every page depends on, children rendered first with both analytics beacons trailing (analytics must not wrap content). (category: tests, source: self, score: 5.4 — impact 9 × ease 6) — RESOLVED this commit (#211): added `src/app/__tests__/layout.test.tsx` with 18 cases across 6 describes, modeled on the OG-route tree-inspection precedent (the component returns `<html>/<body>` which cannot cleanly DOM-render under jsdom, so the test inspects the returned React element tree directly rather than via @testing-library `render`). The two analytics beacons (`@/components/analytics/VercelAnalytics`, `VercelSpeedInsights`) are mocked to stable stub functions via `vi.mock` so the tree walk identifies them by reference; `@/lib/seo`'s `siteConfig` is left **real** so a `baseUrl` regression surfaces here tied to source, not a magic string. metadata (6 cases: `metadataBase` is a URL === `new URL(siteConfig.baseUrl).href` + origin `https://tiered.tv`; `title.default` + `title.template`; description names spoiler-free + Editor's Canon + Community Rank; `applicationName`; RSS alternate has one entry at `/feed.xml`; favicon set SVG-first + `.ico`/`icon-32`/`icon-192` + apple-touch-icon). viewport (2 cases: `themeColor === '#0E0B08'`; `width`/`initialScale`). html shell (2 cases: `lang='en'` + `suppressHydrationWarning`; exactly `<head>` then `<body>`). theme bootstrap (5 cases: reads `tiered_theme`; applies `dataset.theme=t`; gates on `'light'||'dark'`; wrapped in `try{`/`}catch(e){}`; is a self-invoking IIFE). body shell (3 cases: `flex min-h-dvh flex-col` className; children first then both beacons in order; each beacon mounted exactly once). No source change. `src/app/layout.tsx` is not a URL (shipped phase 1) so no e2e fixture row is owed. After this, the next testless `src/app/` files are the page-level routes (`(default)/mod/page.tsx` — RBAC mod queue, `(default)/u/[handle]/page.tsx` — profile w/ Supabase + Auth0, `internal/rank-shift-demo/page.tsx` — internal demo, `(default)/privacy/page.tsx` + `terms/page.tsx` — markdown legal, `(default)/themes/[theme]/page.tsx`, `shows/[show]/page.tsx`, `shows/[show]/season/[slug]/page.tsx`) and the three layout files (`(default)/layout.tsx`, `shows/[show]/layout.tsx`).

- [x] [MED] `src/app/(default)/sign-in/page.tsx` ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` route after the #131–#193 drain pinned every `src/app/api/` route handler plus `src/middleware.ts`, `src/app/sitemap.ts`, `src/app/robots.ts`, `src/app/feed.xml/route.ts`, `src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts`, every `*/opengraph-image.tsx` variant, the root `src/app/opengraph-image.tsx`, and the phase-33 page-level `permanentRedirect` pair (canon #180 + community #193). `/sign-in` is the magic-link entry point — the only auth-flow page in the product. 70 lines composing five behaviors none of which a hermetic e2e walk pins from the outside (the e2e walk needs an anon session, hits `/sign-in`, asserts the form is reachable; it cannot pin the signed-in redirect branch nor `noIndex` nor the `encodeURIComponent` discipline on the loginHref query string): (1) **Segment-config export** (`src/app/(default)/sign-in/page.tsx:7`) — `dynamic = 'force-dynamic'` — the route reads per-viewer auth state; baking it static would serve one viewer's session to everyone. (2) **`generateMetadata` + `noIndex` discipline** (`src/app/(default)/sign-in/page.tsx:9-16`) — title `'Sign in'`, description names magic link by email, path `/sign-in`, `noIndex: true`. The `noIndex` is load-bearing: sign-in pages have zero search value, indexing them dilutes the home's PageRank and produces noisy SERP entries. A regression dropping `noIndex` would let Googlebot index the sign-in flow — the SEO equivalent of indexing `/api/`. (3) **Signed-in redirect branch** (`src/app/(default)/sign-in/page.tsx:23-29`) — when `auth0.getSession()` returns a user, `redirect(returnTo)` fires. The redirect target is the bare returnTo, NOT the loginHref (would loop the magic-link flow); `next/navigation.redirect` (302) is appropriate for a session-scoped bounce, not `permanentRedirect`. (4) **`returnTo` default + URL encoding** (`src/app/(default)/sign-in/page.tsx:24-25,31`) — `returnTo = params.return ?? '/'` (the default is home, not undefined which would yield `?returnTo=undefined`). `loginHref = '/auth/login?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent(returnTo)}'` — the `encodeURIComponent` is load-bearing: a regression to raw interpolation would corrupt the query string on any returnTo containing `&`, `?`, `=`, `%`, or unicode (e.g. `/shows/survivor?view=community` would lose the `view=community` slice on the Auth0 round-trip). (5) **Form + Link contract** (`src/app/(default)/sign-in/page.tsx:48-63`) — form `action={loginHref}` + `method="get"`; the Continue Link `href={loginHref}` + `prefetch={false}` (the auth flow must not prefetch — it would consume the magic link); the three data-testids (`sign-in-page` / `sign-in-form` / `sign-in-continue`) are the hooks the e2e auth spec keys off. Segment-config (1 case: `dynamic === 'force-dynamic'`). generateMetadata (4 cases: title === 'Sign in'; description names "magic link" + "email"; canonical === `https://tiered.tv/sign-in`; robots === `{index: false, follow: false}` — pins noIndex). Signed-in redirect (5 cases: session.user + `?return=/foo` → redirect('/foo'); session.user + no return → redirect('/'); searchParams undefined → redirect('/'); the redirect target is the bare returnTo, NOT the loginHref; redirect called exactly once on the signed-in path). Session-shape branch detection (3 cases: null session → no redirect, form renders; empty `{}` session → no redirect — the gate is `session?.user`, not `session`; `getSession` called exactly once per request — no auth fan-out). Signed-out rendering, loginHref construction (5 cases: searchParams empty → `'/auth/login?returnTo=%2F'`; searchParams undefined → same; simple return path encoded as `'%2Fshows%2Fsurvivor'`; special chars (`& ? = %`) all survive intact through the query string with `decodeURIComponent` round-trip; unicode encoded as UTF-8 + percent (Société → `Soci%C3%A9t%C3%A9`) — defends against a regression to `encodeURI` (which leaves `? & =` un-encoded and would corrupt `/shows/<slug>?view=community` returnTos). Form + Link contract (6 cases: all three testids present; form action mirrors the Continue link href exactly; form method === 'get'; aria-label === 'Sign in with email'; h1 reads 'Sign in'; Continue link text reads 'Continue with email'). No source change. `/sign-in` is not a new URL (shipped phase 10) so no e2e fixture row is owed — the path already appears in `apps/e2e/src/fixtures/canonical-urls.ts` and the existing auth spec covers it at the network level. After this, the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` routes are the heavier server-component pages (`src/app/(default)/mod/page.tsx` — RBAC-gated mod queue, `src/app/(default)/u/[handle]/page.tsx` — user profile with Supabase reads + Auth0 owner-detection, `src/app/internal/rank-shift-demo/page.tsx` — internal demo, `src/app/(default)/privacy/page.tsx` + `terms/page.tsx` — markdown-backed legal pages, then the three heavy content pages `shows/[show]/page.tsx`, `themes/[theme]/page.tsx`, and `season/[slug]/page.tsx`). - [x] [MED] `src/app/shows/[show]/community/page.tsx` ships without a colocated test — the second half of the phase-33 page-level `permanentRedirect` pair (canon was the first, pinned in #180 / `1ce476e`). The standalone Community Rank page was consolidated into the show page, so this route 308s every legacy `/shows/<slug>/community` URL into the consolidated show page on the community pane via `/shows/<slug>?view=community`. 19 lines composing three behaviors none of which a hermetic e2e walk pins from the outside (e2e walks that the redirect lands; it cannot pin that the redirect uses 308 vs 302 nor that a future regression dropped the `?view=community` query): (1) **`generateStaticParams`** (`src/app/shows/[show]/community/page.tsx:13-15`) — returns one row per `getAllShows()` show with the bracket-segment-shaped key `{ show: <slug> }`. A regression to `{ slug: ... }` would render zero static params and the route would fall through to on-demand SSR, defeating SSG. (2) **Redirect target with REQUIRED query string** (`src/app/shows/[show]/community/page.tsx:18`) — `permanentRedirect('/shows/${params.show}?view=community')`. The query string is load-bearing here, inverse of the canon redirect: canon is the default pane on the consolidated show page, but community needs the explicit `?view=community` flag. A regression dropping the query would land every legacy community URL on the canon pane and silently bleed community-page SEO authority onto the wrong destination; appending a trailing slash before the `?` would violate the locked `/shows/<slug>` URL contract. (3) **Redirect mechanism** (`src/app/shows/[show]/community/page.tsx:18`) — `permanentRedirect` (308) rather than `redirect` (302). The behavioral difference is invisible to a Playwright follow-redirect walk (both land on the same page), but search engines treat them very differently: 308 transfers PageRank to the new URL, 302 does not. A regression `permanentRedirect` → `redirect` would silently drain accumulated SEO authority off the `/shows/<slug>/community` URL family. (category: tests, source: self, score: 4.5) — RESOLVED this commit (#193): added `src/app/shows/[show]/community/__tests__/page.test.tsx` with 12 cases across 3 describes, modeled on the #180 canon precedent — `@/content` (`getAllShows`) + `next/navigation` (`permanentRedirect`) mocked via `vi.hoisted` + `vi.mock` so each test drives every branch deterministically and captures the redirect target. `permanentRedirect` is mocked as a thrower (`NEXT_REDIRECT` error) matching the runtime behavior, typed `(_url: string): never` so vitest infers the captured-call shape correctly. `generateStaticParams` (4 cases: returns one row per loaded show with the `[show]` bracket-segment-shaped key; key shape pinned as `{ show: <slug> }` defending against a regression to `{ slug }` that would render zero static params; empty-shows path returns `[]` — defensive against a content-layer reset; `getAllShows` called exactly once — no loader fan-out). Target URL (6 cases: redirects to `/shows/<slug>?view=community`; slug passed verbatim — no transformation, no kebab-from-name; multi-token slugs preserved intact via `rupauls-drag-race`; MUST append `?view=community` — the inverse of canon's NO-query rule, defended with positive (`/\?view=community$/`) + negative (`/\?view=canon/`) assertions; NO trailing slash before the `?` — violates the locked `/shows/<slug>` URL contract; never a self-loop `/community$` and never the bare `/shows/<slug>` (the canon pane, the wrong default)). Redirect mechanism (2 cases: `permanentRedirect` invoked exactly once — pins 308 over 302, defending the SEO-authority transfer that a Playwright follow-redirect walk cannot see; exactly one call per request — no double-redirect, no fan-out). No source change. `/shows/<show>/community` is not a new URL (shipped phase 8, consolidated by phase 33a 3ac0b42) so no e2e fixture row is owed — the redirect is already wired into the build's SSG output and the canonical-urls + redirects fixtures track the 308 landing. After this, the phase-33 redirect pair is fully colocated-test-pinned. The next-by-impact testless `src/app/` route shifts from the SSG redirect family to page-level routes (`src/app/(default)/u/[handle]/page.tsx`, `src/app/shows/[show]/page.tsx`, `src/app/(default)/themes/[theme]/page.tsx`, etc) — each materially heavier to test (server components reading content + Supabase + auth state) and a different precedent class from the OG + redirect drain. The behavioral difference is invisible to a Playwright follow-redirect walk (both land on the same page), but search engines treat them very differently: 308 transfers PageRank to the new URL, 302 does not. A regression `permanentRedirect` → `redirect` would silently drain accumulated SEO authority off the `/shows/<slug>/canon` URL family. - [x] [MED] `src/app/opengraph-image.tsx` ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` route after the 15-tick #131–#175 drain pinned every `src/app/api/` route handler plus `src/middleware.ts`, `src/app/sitemap.ts`, `src/app/robots.ts`, `src/app/feed.xml/route.ts`, `src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts`, `src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx`, `src/app/shows/[show]/opengraph-image.tsx`, and `src/app/themes/[theme]/opengraph-image.tsx`. `app/opengraph-image.tsx` is the root-level OG share card every page on tiered.tv inherits when the destination route does not carve its own `opengraph-image.tsx` — `/`, `/themes`, `/shows`, `/about`, `/sign-in`, `/u/[handle]`, `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/internal/*`, every social share whose URL bottoms out at the app root layout. 52 lines composing four behaviors none of which a hermetic e2e walk pins from the outside (e2e walks that the OG URL is reachable, not the pixel content): (1) **Segment-config exports** (`src/app/opengraph-image.tsx:3-6`) — `runtime = 'edge'` (the root OG is intentionally edge since it reads no content layer — per-surface variants declare `'nodejs'` because they touch file-system loaders; a regression unifying them to `'nodejs'` would still build but lose the edge characteristic on the highest-traffic share surface), `alt = 'tiered.tv — the seasons, ranked. no spoilers.'` (the verbatim brand promise CLAUDE.md "Tone of voice" reserves — crawlers + screen readers surface this string on every default-OG share), `size = {width: 1200, height: 630}` (the OpenGraph 1.91:1 ratio Twitter + Facebook key cache off), `contentType = 'image/png'`. (2) **ImageResponse construction** (`src/app/opengraph-image.tsx:8-52`) — the route directly constructs a `new ImageResponse(jsx, {...size})` and returns it (no fan-out, no double-render); a regression that wrapped the response or constructed multiple instances per call would break the build's static OG image pipeline. (3) **Brand text content** (`src/app/opengraph-image.tsx:25,35,45`) — three rendered text spans: `"tiered.tv"` (wordmark), `"the seasons, ranked."` (promise-first), `"no spoilers."` (promise-second), rendered in that document order — splitting the promise across two lines is intentional editorial register (beat-pause between offer and constraint). (4) **Brand palette** (`src/app/opengraph-image.tsx:14,20,24,31,41`) — five hand-rolled hex values: paper `#0E0B08`, inherited ink `#F2EADB`, wordmark gold `#E8B65A`, bright ink `#D8CFBE` (offer line), muted ink `#9D9485` (constraint line) — the root OG is brand-anchored not show-anchored, so the palette is hardcoded here rather than threaded from `--show-*` tokens; a regression that drifted any of the five hexes would visually corrupt the share card on every default-OG share. The card's layout shape — flex column, items flex-start, content justified center, 140px wordmark + 56px couplet — is the editorial-block register; a regression to `alignItems: 'center'` would re-cast the brand mark as a stamp and break the editorial voice. - [x] [MED] `src/app/themes/[theme]/opengraph-image.tsx` ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` route after the 14-tick #131–#172 drain pinned every `src/app/api/` route handler plus `src/middleware.ts`, `src/app/sitemap.ts`, `src/app/robots.ts`, `src/app/feed.xml/route.ts`, `src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts`, `src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx`, and `src/app/shows/[show]/opengraph-image.tsx`. `app/themes/[theme]/opengraph-image.tsx` is the per-themed-list social-share card every link to `/themes/<theme>` hits from Slack, iMessage, Twitter, Facebook, etc — the discovery surface for all 12 themed lists in the catalog. 21 lines composing four behaviors none of which a hermetic e2e walk pins from the outside (e2e walks that the OG URL is reachable, not the pixel content): (1) **Single `notFound()` branch** (`src/app/themes/[theme]/opengraph-image.tsx:15`) — unknown theme → `notFound()`. A regression that fell through with `theme: null` would 500 on the `theme.title`/`theme.description` reads downstream. (2) **Static eyebrow + title/description pass-through** (`src/app/themes/[theme]/opengraph-image.tsx:17-19`) — `eyebrow: 'tiered.tv · Themed list'` is intentionally static (every list share card reads the same wayfinder — a regression to a derived string would let one list's eyebrow drift while the others stayed canonical); `title: theme.title` (not `theme.slug` — a kebab slug `best-premieres` reads wrong as headline copy on a 1200×630 share card); `blurb: theme.description` verbatim from frontmatter (the curator-authored editorial subtitle). (3) **No palette pass-through** — themed lists ship in ceremonial gold per the `/themes` design (no per-show paint), so the route hands `{eyebrow, title, blurb}` to `buildOgImage` with NO palette key. A regression that started spreading a palette would let an unrelated theme field pollute the OG factory. (4) **Segment-config exports** (`src/app/themes/[theme]/opengraph-image.tsx:5-8`) — `runtime = 'nodejs'` (load-bearing: a regression to `'edge'` like the root `/opengraph-image.tsx` shape would 500 every theme OG card at build time because the route reads from the file-system content loaders), `alt = 'tiered.tv — the seasons, ranked. no spoilers.'` (the literal string crawlers + screen readers surface), `size = {width: 1200, height: 630}` (the OpenGraph 1.91:1 ratio Twitter + Facebook key cache off), `contentType = 'image/png'`. The underlying `buildOgImage` template is exhaustively tested in `src/lib/og/__tests__/template.test.tsx`; the route handler's own contracts — single notFound branch, static eyebrow, title + blurb pass-through, no palette key, segment-config exports — are dark to e2e. Segment-config exports (`runtime === 'nodejs'` — pinning the contrast against the root `/opengraph-image.tsx`'s `'edge'` runtime, defending against a build-time 500 if a regression unified them; `size === {width: 1200, height: 630}` — the OpenGraph 1.91:1 ratio Twitter + Facebook key cache off; `contentType === 'image/png'`; `alt` exactly `'tiered.tv — the seasons, ranked. no spoilers.'`). notFound branch (null theme → `notFound()` called once + `buildOgImage` *never* invoked; undefined theme treated identically — defensive against loader signature drift between `Theme | null` and `Theme | undefined`; happy path → `notFound` never called). Content-layer resolution (`getTheme` called with the URL theme slug verbatim — no transformation; `getTheme` called exactly once per request — no loader fan-out). Eyebrow + title + blurb pass-through (eyebrow exactly `'tiered.tv · Themed list'` — the literal copy every list card surfaces; title is `theme.title` verbatim, not the URL slug, defended with a `best-finales`→`Best Finales` divergence case; blurb is `theme.description` verbatim, no fallback synthesis — even a very short `'One.'` passes through). Happy-path invariants (`buildOgImage` called exactly once per request — no fan-out / no double-render; route returns the value `buildOgImage` produces — it is a pure composer; the argument shape is exactly `{blurb, eyebrow, title}` — no extras, none missing; the call object has NO `palette` key — themed lists are palette-less, a regression that started spreading palette through would let an unrelated theme field pollute the OG factory). No source change. `/themes/[theme]/opengraph-image` is not a new URL (shipped phase 17) so no e2e fixture row is owed — the route family is already wired into the build's static OG image pipeline. After this, the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` route is `src/app/opengraph-image.tsx` (the root 52-line static fallback — `runtime = 'edge'` rather than `'nodejs'`).
  - issue: #175

- [x] [MED] `src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx` ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` route after the 13-tick #131–#146 drain pinned every `src/app/api/` route handler (#131 `/api/auth/me`, #132 `/api/internal/recompute`, #134 `/api/vote`, #135 `/api/comment`, #136 `/api/comments`, #137 `/api/ranking/[show]`, #140 `/api/flag`, #141 `/api/mod/action`) plus `src/middleware.ts` (#142), `src/app/sitemap.ts` (#143), `src/app/robots.ts` (#144), `src/app/feed.xml/route.ts` (#145), and `src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts` (#146). `app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx` is the per-season social-share card every link to `/shows/<show>/season/<slug>` hits from Slack, iMessage, Twitter, Facebook, etc — the discovery surface that drives traffic for **298 season slugs** across the catalog. 46 lines composing six behaviors none of which a hermetic e2e walk pins from the outside (e2e walks the OG URL is reachable, not the pixel content): (1) **Two `notFound()` branches** (`src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx:24,26`) — unknown show → `notFound()`; known show + unknown season → `notFound()`. A regression that fell through with `show: undefined` would 500 on the `show.palette` read; a regression that 200'd on an unknown season would render an empty card with a missing title. (2) **`padRank` helper** (`src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx:12-15`) — `null/undefined → ''`, single-digit `n` → `#0n`, two-digit `n` → `#nn`, three-digit `n` → `#nnn`. A regression to non-padded would emit `#1` instead of `#01` on every top-of-canon share card; a regression to fixed-width pad would emit `#001` and break the visual rhythm. (3) **Canon-rank lookup + three-step fallback chain** (`src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx:28-30`) — `getCanon(showSlug)?.entries.find(e => e.season === season.number)?.rank ?? season.canonical_position ?? null`. The chain pins four branches: canon-file rank wins over `canonical_position`; null canon falls back to `season.canonical_position`; canon-file with no matching entry falls back to `season.canonical_position`; neither set → null. The lookup matches on `season.number` (not title / slug) — a regression matching on the wrong field would label every season `#01` because the top-ranked season is always at index 0. (4) **Eyebrow conditional** (`src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx:31-33`) — truthy canonRank → `"Tiers / ${show.name} · Editor's Canon ${padRank(rank)}"`; falsy → `"Tiers / ${show.name} · Season ${season.number}"`. The two strings are the literal copy every social card surfaces in the eyebrow row. (5) **Blurb fallback chain** (`src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx:38-39`) — `season.lede ?? season.tag ?? \`${show.name}, season ${season.number}.\``. The lede is the curator's hand-authored season opener — preferring it over `tag` (a short marketing-style label) is load-bearing for editorial voice; a regression swapping the order would surface "all-stars" or "second chances" where "The all-stars season the format spent twenty years earning." should appear. The composed fallback uses `show.name` (not the URL slug — kebab slugs read wrong as copy). (6) **Palette pass-through + segment-config exports** (`src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/opengraph-image.tsx:5-8,40-44`) — `show.palette.{paper,ink,primary}` extracted into the `buildOgImage({palette})` argument (no spread of the whole palette object, so future palette siblings don't pollute the OG factory); the four Next-required segment-config exports (`runtime = 'nodejs'`, `alt`, `size: {width: 1200, height: 630}`, `contentType = 'image/png'`). The `runtime = 'nodejs'` is load-bearing — the route reads from the content layer (file-system loaders) so a regression to `'edge'` (the root `/opengraph-image.tsx` shape) would 500 every season OG card at build time. The underlying `buildOgImage` template is exhaustively tested in `src/lib/og/__tests__/template.test.tsx` (mocked `next/og` ImageResponse, captured tree walk for tokens); the route handler's own contracts — eyebrow conditional, padRank, blurb fallback chain, two notFound branches, palette extraction, segment-config exports — are dark to e2e. - [x] [MED] `src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts` ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` route after the 12-tick #131–#145 drain pinned every `src/app/api/` route handler plus `src/middleware.ts` (#142), `src/app/sitemap.ts` (#143), `src/app/robots.ts` (#144), and `src/app/feed.xml/route.ts` (#145). `app/feed/[slug]/route.ts` is the per-show RSS 2.0 feed (phase 32) — the discovery surface every RSS reader hits at `/feed/<show>.xml` for each of the 13 shows the catalog tracks. 45 lines composing five behaviors none of which a hermetic e2e walk pins from the outside: (1) **`.xml`-suffix discipline** (`src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts:27-28`) — `slug` is a single dynamic segment of shape `<show>.xml` (the only App Router shape that can serve a `.xml`-suffixed dynamic path; a folder cannot mix `[show]` and a literal `.xml`); `if (!slug.endsWith('.xml')) return notFound()` defends against a slug like `survivor` (no suffix) returning a 200 with an unintended feed — a regression to `startsWith` or no check would emit RSS for the wrong path shape; the slice `slug.slice(0, -'.xml'.length)` must produce the bare show slug for lookup. (2) **Two 404 branches** (`src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts:17-20`, `27-31`) — the shared `notFound()` returns `Response('feed not found', { status: 404, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' } })` for non-`.xml` slugs, unknown shows, and `buildShowFeedItems()` returning `null`. The 404 must NOT emit `application/rss+xml` (RSS readers would log a parse error against the empty body), the body is the literal `'feed not found'` string, and the path must short-circuit before `renderRss` is invoked. (3) **`generateStaticParams`** (`src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts:12-14`) — maps `getAllShows()` to `{ slug: '<show>.xml' }`, one entry per show; the build pre-renders 13 per-show feeds at SSG time. A regression returning `{ slug: show.slug }` (no `.xml`) would emit `/feed/<show>` static pages and break the discovery URL contract; missing the function entirely would force per-request rendering. (4) **Channel metadata** (`src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts:34-39`) — the channel object passed to `renderRss`: title = `"${show.name} — every season ranked, no spoilers"` (per-show variant of the brand promise), link = `canonicalUrl('/shows/${show.slug}')` (the show home, not the feed itself — the "the human page the feed describes" semantic from `FeedChannel`), feedUrl = `canonicalUrl('/feed/${show.slug}.xml')` (atom:self — a relative or wrong path would have RSS readers refresh from the wrong origin), description names the show by name and the two content surfaces (season pages + canon revisions). (5) **Items composition + ISR contract** (`src/app/feed/[slug]/route.ts:30,42-44,9-10`) — `buildShowFeedItems(showSlug)` invoked exactly once per request, output forwarded untouched to `renderRss`; `Content-Type: application/rss+xml; charset=utf-8` for 200s; `dynamic = 'force-static'` + `revalidate = 3600` (hourly window matching `app/sitemap.ts` and `app/feed.xml/route.ts`). The underlying helpers (`getAllShows`, `getShow`, `buildShowFeedItems`, `renderRss`) are exhaustively tested in `src/content/__tests__/` + `src/lib/feed/__tests__/`; the route handler's own contracts are dark to e2e. Items composition (items forwarded untouched in order — the array passed to `renderRss` is the same object reference returned by `buildShowFeedItems`; empty items array → 200 + `renderRss` still called once; `buildShowFeedItems` called with the bare show slug (no `.xml`) exactly once per request — pins both the slug-stripping and the "no per-render fan-out" disciplines). ISR contract (`dynamic` exports as `'force-static'` — a regression to per-request would burn the loaders on every RSS poll; `revalidate` exports as `3600` — the hourly window matching `app/sitemap.ts` and `app/feed.xml/route.ts`). - [x] [MED] `src/app/feed.xml/route.ts` ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact testless `src/app/` route after the 11-tick #131–#144 drain pinned every `src/app/api/` route handler plus `src/middleware.ts` (#142), `src/app/sitemap.ts` (#143), and `src/app/robots.ts` (#144). `app/feed.xml/route.ts` is the global RSS 2.0 feed (phase 32) — the discovery surface every RSS reader hits at the canonical absolute URL. 24 lines composing four behaviors none of which a hermetic e2e walk pins from the outside: (1) **Content-Type discipline** (`src/app/feed.xml/route.ts:22`) — `application/rss+xml; charset=utf-8` is the MIME RSS readers expect; a regression to `text/xml` / `application/xml` still parses but loses the RSS-specific signal feed-discovery clients (and the Atom-style `<link rel="alternate">` we emit elsewhere) key off. (2) **Channel metadata** (`src/app/feed.xml/route.ts:11-20`) — the channel object passed to `renderRss`: title = `"${siteConfig.name} — the seasons, ranked. no spoilers."` (the brand promise; pinned against drift in `siteConfig.name` in a single place), link = `canonicalUrl('/')` (absolute home), feedUrl = `canonicalUrl('/feed.xml')` (atom:self pointer — a relative or wrong path would have RSS readers refresh from the wrong origin), description naming the three content surfaces (seasons / canon / themed lists). (3) **Items composition** (`src/app/feed.xml/route.ts:19`) — `buildGlobalFeedItems()` invoked exactly once per request, output forwarded untouched to `renderRss`; the route must not 500 on an empty content drain. The trailing slash on `/api/` is load-bearing — `/api` (no slash) would block `/apiary`, `/api-docs`, and any future `/api`-prefixed page family; the bare `/mod` correctly blocks `/mod` AND `/mod/*` (the queue surface where pending comments would expose author handles + flagged content); `/sign-in` blocks the Auth0 sign-in flow, a no-content redirect surface that has zero search value. Drop any one path from this list and a private surface gets indexed — the mod-queue exposure is the loudest. (2) **`userAgent: '*'` + `allow: '/'`** — open to all crawlers on every path except the disallow set; a regression that tightened `allow` would silently de-index the entire site. (3) **`sitemap: canonicalUrl('/sitemap.xml')`** — points crawlers to the absolute sitemap URL `https://tiered.tv/sitemap.xml`; a regression returning the raw path would emit a relative URL crawlers cannot follow. (4) **`host: siteConfig.baseUrl`** — the bare baseUrl `'https://tiered.tv'` (NOT canonicalUrl-wrapped — different shape from `sitemap`); a regression that ran `canonicalUrl` here would append a trailing slash `'https://tiered.tv/'` and break Yandex's strict `Host:` directive parser. The `host` vs `sitemap` shape distinction is the kind of detail typecheck cannot enforce (both are strings) and e2e cannot exercise (Next.js serializes the same way), so a unit test is the only structural defense. The route loaders (`getSitemapRoutes`, `getFeedPaths`) already have exhaustive coverage in `src/lib/__tests__/routes.test.ts` (the exclude/include rules, the `/u/[handle]` env gate, the per-show feed slug-alignment + order discipline), but the **consumer transformation** that turns those route lists into the sitemap shape was never pinned: (1) **priority differentiation** — home (`pattern === '/'`) → 1.0; non-home pages → 0.7; feed entries → 0.4; a regression keying priority off `route.path === '/'` instead of `route.pattern === '/'` keys home correctly for the current route set but breaks the pattern discipline that lets future static-route additions reuse `/` as a literal path under a different pattern; (4) **changeFrequency: 'weekly'** on every entry — a regression to `'daily'` would inflate the recrawl budget (and trip rate-limits at some crawlers); (5) **single `Date` lastModified per emission** — one `const now = new Date()` shared across every entry; malformed shapes each return `400 invalid_body`; the parse gate runs *before* session resolution; (2) **auth gate** — `/api/flag` requires auth (anon members cannot flag), so an anon caller and a session with no `sub` each return `401 auth_required` with `flagComment` never reached; the anon cookie regex + `isValidAnonId` UUID validation still run for genuine since the resolved anon id is passed to `claimAnonSession` so prior anon activity is re-attributed; an authed caller lazily `upsertUser`s + `claimAnonSession`s and flags as the **claimed** session id (not the raw anon id); (3) **handle derivation** — `handleFromSession` falls through `nickname` (strip leading `@+`, lowercase) → email-localpart (lowercase) → sub-sanitized (`[^a-z0-9-]→-`, slice 32, lowercase) → "user", same multi-source fallback `/api/comment` uses because magic-link sessions don't always carry a `nickname`; (4) **snake_case success shape** — the response echoes `{ok, flag_count, auto_hidden}` with deliberately *snake_case* keys, distinct from the `{flagCount, autoHidden}` shape `flagComment` returns; a regression that spread the helper result (`...result`) or returned `flagCount`/`autoHidden` would silently break the client's auto-hide affordance even though no type would fail; (5) **RPC error mapping** — `42501` (RLS) → `401 auth_required`, `22023` → `400 invalid_input`, anything else → `500 rpc_failed`; (6) **auth-resolve failure** — a thrown `auth0.getSession()`, a thrown `upsertUser`, or a thrown `claimAnonSession` each map to `500 auth_resolve_failed` (the entire session-resolution try/catch); (7) **route contract** — `runtime === 'nodejs'`, `dynamic === 'force-dynamic'`. A regression in any of these branches passes a hermetic e2e walk but would either let an anon caller flag with no auth, drop the auto-hide signal silently, or mis-derive the user handle. phase 35's "vote → refresh shows true persisted net" acceptance criterion makes the route a regression-bearing surface for the three user-reported vote bugs it closed. `apps/e2e/tests/ranking-api.spec.ts` walks the route, but a hermetic e2e walk only ever drives the happy path against the live `getCommunityRanking` (canon-mirror fallback when the seed votes are absent), so the route's own four contracts are dark to it: (1) **the entry-shape transformation** — the route projects each `CommunityRankRow` to a *public* shape `{rank, seasonNumber, seasonSlug, title, tag, score, approval, voteCount, trend}` that drops the full internal `Season` object (a regression that spread `...e.season` or returned the row verbatim would leak internal frontmatter — `body_md`, `lede`, `pull`, `canonical_position`, every editorial draft field — to every show-page poll); (2) **the top-level response envelope** — `{ok, show, source, votersThisWeek, lastRecomputeAt, version, generatedAt}` keys; a regression that dropped `source` or `version` would break the client's trend / canon-mirror gating; `generatedAt` is a round-trippable ISO string; (3) **the 404 unknown-show branch** — `getShow(slug) → null` short-circuits to `{ok:false,error:'unknown_show'}` with `getCommunityRanking` *never* reached (the validation gate runs before the DB read); (4) **the loader pass-through** — `getCommunityRanking(show, seasons, canon)` is called with the *resolved* show plus its `getAllSeasons(show.slug)` + `getCanon(show.slug)` outputs verbatim (a regression that passed the URL slug instead of the resolved `show` would invalidate the canon-mirror fallback's tag derivation); and (5) **the `dynamic = 'force-dynamic'` contract** — a static bake would freeze one show's ranking into the route at build time and serve stale data on every poll, reintroducing exactly the staleness bug the route header explains the design rejected ISR caching to avoid; the next-15-route async-params await is also load-bearing under Next.js 15. A regression in any of these branches passes a hermetic e2e walk but would either leak internal season frontmatter to every public viewer, freeze the ranking, or 500 instead of 404'ing an unknown slug. - [x] [MED] `src/app/api/comment/route.ts` ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact route-handler test gap after #131 pinned `/api/auth/me`, #132 pinned `/api/internal/recompute`, and #134 pinned `/api/vote`. `/api/comment` is the comment write path — every season page's `CommentInput` posts here — and it is the **only** path through which the OpenAI moderation pre-filter (`moderateComment`) runs before user copy hits the database. Spoiler discipline is P0 (CLAUDE.md "Five hard rules" + agents.md §7), so the verdict-pass-through contract is load-bearing. `apps/e2e/tests/comments.spec.ts` walks happy path + spoiler-blocked + flag flow, but the route's full branch tree is dark to it: (1) **body validation (Zod)** — `targetType` enum (`'season' | 'comment'`), `targetId` length bounds (1–128), `body` length bounds (1–4000), optional nullable UUID `parentId`; malformed shapes each return `400 invalid_body`; the parse gate runs *before* session resolution; (2) **auth gate** — `/api/comment` requires auth (unlike `/api/vote`), so an anon caller and a session with no `sub` each return `401 auth_required` with `postComment` never reached; the anon cookie regex + `isValidAnonId` UUID validation still run for genuine since the resolved anon id is passed to `claimAnonSession` so prior anon activity is re-attributed; an authed caller lazily `upsertUser`s + `claimAnonSession`s and posts as the **claimed** session id (not the raw anon id); (3) **handle derivation** — `handleFromSession` falls through `nickname` (strip leading `@+`, lowercase) → email-localpart (lowercase) → sub-sanitized (`[^a-z0-9-]→-`, slice 32, lowercase) → "user", distinct from `/api/vote`'s simpler nickname-only path because magic-link sessions don't always carry a `nickname`; (4) **verdict pass-through (spoiler discipline)** — `moderateComment(body)` runs exactly once on the parsed body string and *every* verdict field (`verdict`, `categories`, `confidence`, `reason`, `redacted_phrase`) passes through to `postComment` verbatim; a regression that drops `categories` or downgrades a `block` to `allow` would let spoilers slip past the moderation layer; (5) **verdict echo in success body** — the response echoes `verdict` alongside `id`, `status`, `count` so the client can render the held-for-review affordance when `status === 'pending'`; (6) **RPC error mapping** — `42501` (RLS) → `401 auth_required`, `23505` → `429 rate_limited` (new-account 5-comment hold + rate limit), `22023` → `400 invalid_input`, anything else → `500 rpc_failed`; (7) **auth-resolve failure** — a thrown `auth0.getSession()` maps to `500 auth_resolve_failed`; (8) **route contract** — `runtime === 'nodejs'`, `dynamic === 'force-dynamic'`. A regression in any of these branches passes a hermetic e2e walk but would either leak unmoderated text to the published thread, drop the auth gate, misroute a rate-limit to a 500, or mis-derive the user handle. - [x] [MED] `src/app/api/internal/recompute/route.ts` ships without a colocated test — the next-by-impact route-handler test gap after #131 pinned `/api/auth/me`, and a handler whose production authorization guard is structurally invisible to the hermetic e2e suite. `/api/internal/recompute` is the scheduled-recompute entry point a Vercel Cron hits weekly to call the `recompute_rankings()` `SECURITY DEFINER` service-role RPC (it snapshots the live community ranking into `rank_snapshots`; trend / movers / `RankShiftPill` all derive from those snapshots). Its `authorized(request)` guard is env-conditional: when `CRON_SECRET` is **set** (production — Vercel injects `Authorization: Bearer <CRON_SECRET>` on cron invocations) the header must equal `Bearer <CRON_SECRET>` *exactly*, else 401; when `CRON_SECRET` is **unset** (local dev + hermetic e2e) the route is **open** so the harness can seed a snapshot in-band. `apps/e2e/tests/ranking-api.spec.ts` exercises the route, but the hermetic e2e harness runs with `CRON_SECRET` unset — so e2e only ever walks the *open* branch. The production security branch (`!secret` false → exact-bearer compare → 401 on mismatch) is dark to every test: a regression inverting `if (!secret) return true`, dropping the bearer comparison, or loosening it to a case-insensitive / prefix match would let any unauthenticated caller force a service-role recompute in production and slip past the whole verify gate. The `?show=` param scoping (`p_show: null` when absent/empty), the `rowsWritten` numeric coercion, the RPC-error → 500 mapping, and GET/POST parity were also unpinned. The production guard (set secret → matching `Bearer <secret>` authorizes; absent / wrong-value / Bearer-prefix-missing / lowercase-scheme headers each 401 with the RPC never reached), the open path (unset secret → 200 regardless of header), RPC invocation (`?show=` → `p_show: <slug>`; absent and empty `?show=` → `p_show: null`; `rowsWritten` from `Number(data ?? 0)` — `42 → 42`, `null → 0`; `show` echoes the param-or-null; `recomputedAt` a round-trippable ISO string), error mapping (RPC `{ error }` → 500 `{ok:false,error:'recompute_failed',detail}`; a thrown `serviceRoleClient()` → same 500 shape), and the route contract (`GET` routes through the same path as `POST`; `GET` enforces the guard too; `runtime === 'nodejs'`; `dynamic === 'force-dynamic'`). No source change. `/api/internal/recompute` is not a new URL (shipped phase 35) so no e2e fixture row is owed.
