- [x] [MED] `src/app/api/auth/me/route.ts` ships without a colocated test — a route handler whose security-relevant contracts are invisible to the hermetic e2e suite. `/api/auth/me` is the per-viewer auth-state probe the header island fetches on mount to correct statically-rendered chrome (home, all 13 show pages, all 298 season pages, themes — every SSG/ISR surface). It is exercised only *indirectly*: `apps/e2e/tests/authed-example.spec.ts` asserts the header island's *rendered result* (chrome flips to signed-in), never the route handler itself. The 24-line handler carries three load-bearing contracts no test pinned: (1) the `Cache-Control: private, no-store` response header — the payload is per-viewer, so a shared CDN/proxy cache here would serve one member's `{ signedIn:true, user:{handle} }` to another visitor (a cross-viewer auth-state leak); (2) `export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'` — a static bake would freeze one viewer's state into the route for everyone (same leak class); (3) the `.catch(() => null)` on `auth0.getSession()` — an Auth0 outage must not throw out of the probe the header polls on every route. The hermetic e2e suite runs against `next start` with no CDN, so a dropped `no-store` would never surface there — a colocated unit test is the only place these contracts can be pinned. Session→response mapping (nickname session → signed-in `{ok,signedIn,user}` triple; email-only session → handle from the lowercased local-part; `null` session → `signedIn:false,user:null`; userless session → `signedIn:false`), auth resilience (a rejected `getSession` resolves — `await GET()` does not throw — and returns the signed-out payload, status 200), the caching contract (signed-in AND signed-out responses both carry `cache-control: private, no-store`), and the render contract (`getSession` called exactly once; `dynamic === 'force-dynamic'`). No source change — `next/server`'s `NextResponse` imports and runs cleanly under the vitest jsdom env (first test in the repo to import it). `/api/auth/me` is not a new URL (shipped phase 36) so no e2e fixture row is owed; the route family already has e2e coverage. - [x] [HIGH] season-page vote UX leaks Bayesian-prior internals — optimistic update shows `0.2`/`-0.55` blips before settling, "0.2 votes" on first visit, no voted/unvoting affordance, no graceful load-in, negative-direction phrasing is confusing (#64) (category: bug, source: triage, score: 3.2) — RESOLVED this commit: root cause was VotePair displaying the weighted aggregate `SUM(value*weight)` (a fractional ranking signal) as "net votes". Migration `20260518000001_vote_raw_count.sql` adds `raw_count = SUM(value)` to `cast_vote`/`read_vote`; the `/api/vote` route now sends the clean integer net to the client (weighted value stays internal). VotePair gains `aria-pressed` + persistent `.voted` tint + "click to undo" labels (clear voted/unvoting state), and a `data-hydrated` opacity fade so the number doesn't pop `0 → <X>`. Optimistic ±1 now reconciles exactly against the integer server delta — no more blips. Unit + e2e green. GitHub #64 closed by this commit.
- [x] [MED] season-page comment thread renders empty state — read path not wired to Supabase (#24) (category: bug, source: triage, score: 4.2) — RESOLVED by Phase 36 (e95b019): server-side comment read path (`src/lib/comments/thread.ts` + `CommentThreadLive`) wired into `src/app/shows/[show]/season/[slug]/page.tsx`; published rows visible to all, the author's own `pending` row pinned as "held for review", `hidden`/`removed` never public (spoiler/mod P0 intact). e2e `apps/e2e/tests/comment-read.spec.ts` covers authed-sees-own-held + anon-sees-empty. GitHub #24 closed by this commit.
- [x] [HIGH] auth-chrome gap — Header resolves auth server-side but `/` is SSG so it rendered permanently signed-out (#54) (category: bug, source: triage, score: 5.4) — RESOLVED by Phase 36 (e95b019): auth-state island hydrates from `GET /api/auth/me` so chrome reflects sign-in on every route incl. SSG `/`. `authed-example.spec.ts:30` un-fixme'd in the same commit (now a real signed-in-header assertion). GitHub #54 already closed.
- [x] [HIGH] sign-out broken — `/auth/logout?returnTo=/` sent a relative `returnTo`; Auth0 forwards it verbatim as `post_logout_redirect_uri=%2F` and always rejects a non-absolute URL. Fixed: `HeaderView` builds the logout href from `canonicalUrl('/')` (→ `https://tiered.tv/`), URL-encoded, with a unit test asserting `returnTo` decodes to an absolute https URL. Full resolution still needs the absolute URL added to Auth0 → Allowed Logout URLs (dashboard task noted on #56); the code fix is correct and shippable alone. (#56) (category: bug, source: triage, score: 5.6) — this commit
- [x] [HIGH] show-home season grid sorts by season number, not canon rank (category: bug, source: user-jot, score: 5.6) — STALE: resolved by phase 33a (3ac0b42). - [ ] [HIGH] `content/shows/the-voice/seasons/*.md` (seasons 22-29, 8 files) + `content/shows/the-voice.md` frontmatter carry a confirmed, deep factual corruption — **not** a simple numbering offset — per a dedicated scout verification pass (2026-07-26, ninth zero-ship pass, escalating the eighth pass's initial finding). **Severity raised MED→HIGH**: the corruption produces a currently-LIVE, reader-facing false claim that The Voice has ended. `content/shows/the-voice.md` reads `status: hiatus` and its `blurb`/`tagline`/`card_tagline` state the show "ran... fourteen years... before signing off. The chairs turned one last time"; `content/shows/the-voice/seasons/29-the-finale.md` frames itself as "the final season of a fourteen-year run" / "the show closes." **This is false** — scout-verified against Wikipedia's per-season articles plus NBC Insider/Today.com: real NBC Season 29 aired February 2026 (first-ever 3-coach "Battle of Champions" format, Alexia Jayy winner) and real Season 30 is already confirmed airing September 2026 (announced coaches Levine, Kelly Clarkson, Riley Green, Queen Latifah). The show has not ended. Root cause: NBC ran only one cycle in the 2021-22 broadcast year (confirmed via Hollywood Reporter + TV Series Finale — the only break in the show's otherwise unbroken twice-yearly cadence since 2011); our catalog appears to have assumed an unbroken cadence and inserted a phantom "spring 2022" entry (`22-the-return-pair.md`, premiere_date `2022-02-28` — no such NBC cycle exists), which cascades a +1 offset through later files AND compounds into outright conflated cast lists: `22-the-return-pair.md` (claimed spring 2022) actually describes real Season 22's event (Cabello debut, Blake+Gwen reunite, real premiere Sep 19 2022) dated six months early; `23-the-farewell.md` (claimed Sep 2022, "Shelton's final season") conflates real S22's date with real Season 23's actual event (Niall Horan's debut + Shelton's true farewell, real premiere March 6 2023) with a cast list (Shelton/Gwen/Horan/Cabello) matching neither real season; `24-the-reset.md` ("first season without Shelton," Reba+Chance debut, claimed March 2023) duplicates real S23's premiere date, but real S23 still had Shelton — the true "first without Shelton" is real Season 24 (Sep 2023), and real Season 23 (Shelton's actual on-screen farewell) has **no dedicated file at all**, its facts absorbed into ours 23/24; `25-the-anchor.md` (claimed Sep 2023, Reba/Niall/Legend/Gwen) restates the same real-Season-24 cast/timeframe `24-the-reset.md` already claimed — two of our files independently describing one real season; `26-the-duo.md` = real S25 (Dan+Shay, confirmed pass 8) and `27-the-wild-card.md` = real S26 (Snoop+Bublé) are clean +1-offset cases, no further conflation; `28-the-homecoming.md` (Levine's return, claimed Feb 2025, cast Reba/Bublé/Levine/Snoop) has the right return-date (real Feb 3 2025) but real S27's actual cast was Legend/Bublé/Ballerini/Levine — Kelsea Ballerini's real-simultaneous debut is missing here and instead surfaces alone in `29-the-finale.md` (claimed Sep 2025, Ballerini's debut, framed as the series finale) — but Ballerini's real debut was real S27 (Feb 2025, alongside Levine), not a later fictional "final" season; real Season 28 (Sep 2025, the date our file actually points to) had a completely different cast (Bublé/Reba/Niall Horan/Snoop Dogg) and was explicitly not a finale. Net: 8 of 29 season files carry wrong dates, conflated casts, a duplicated season, an omitted real season (Shelton's true farewell), and a wholly fabricated "series finale" that never happened — repeated as fact in the show's own frontmatter. **Fix is NOT a same-tick patch**: needs a pass that (1) re-verifies each of the 8 affected seasons' cast/date/event against Wikipedia/NBC source-by-source, (2) decides file-count treatment (real S23 has no file at all — may need insertion, shifting every later number), (3) rewrites `content/shows/the-voice.md` `status: hiatus` → `airing` and strips the false "signed off" framing, (4) authors the two real unfiled seasons (real S29 Feb 2026 "Battle of Champions," real S30 Sep 2026), (5) rebases `canon.md` ranks/`canonical_position` across the renumbered range, (6) cross-checks every existing themed-list entry citing `show: the-voice` with season ≥22 for now-wrong references, (7) regenerates e2e canonical-URL fixtures after file renames. Recommend promoting to a dedicated `/expand`-tracked phase or a direct oversight-reviewed tick given the blast radius and the risk of compounding fabrication errors with no review checkpoint in the autonomous loop. Blocking: do not author new the-voice content depending on any specific season number ≥22's real-world event, and do not ship either of the two Rule-3 concepts blocked at the eighth pass, until this resolves. (category: bug, source: self, score: 4.8 — impact 8 × ease 6, raised from 2.5 given the confirmed live false editorial claim) — filed 2026-07-26 during a cloud /march tick's Rule 3 research pass (eighth pass); escalated same-day (ninth pass) with full scout-verified season-by-season findings — see `plan/LISTS.md` Ideas log for both entries. **Addendum (2026-07-31, cloud march tick, self-observation):** the blocking instruction above was already violated once, same-day, prior to this observation — commit 9ec927d9 (`the-panel-turned-over-more-than-the-contestants-did` extend) added a "The Voice S23 'The Farewell'" entry citing "Blake Shelton's twelfth and final season, the last founding coach's departure" — this restates the exact fabricated claim this row documents as corrupted (`23-the-farewell.md` conflates two different real seasons and misdates Shelton's actual on-screen farewell). Flagging here rather than reverting — a revert mid-ledger risks its own rank-renumbering error without the dedicated fix pass this row already calls for. This tick's own pick avoided `the-voice` ≥22 entirely; future Rule 3 ticks must keep excluding it until the dedicated fix lands. **Mirrored to GitHub 2026-08-08 (cloud march tick, fifty-third pass):** filed as issue #762 (unlabeled) — per the escape route the fifty-first pass identified (mirrored issues route around this row's own Step 3b.5 precedence via march's Step 1 triage gate, same mechanism that unstuck the two CRITIQUE HIGH findings as #759/#760), so a future triage-gated tick can pick this up despite the standing content-gap row blocking `/iterate` per issue #758. **Triage update (2026-08-08, cloud march tick):** labeled `triage:needs-user`, not `loop-queued` — deferring to this row's own recommendation above (oversight-reviewed tick given blast radius/fabrication risk), rather than the #759/#760 precedent, since those two were narrow code fixes and this is an 8-file renumbering cascade with a canon rebase and a themed-list cross-reference sweep. - [ ] [HIGH] The night shift (`.github/workflows/night.yml`, `/digest`) silently failed to produce a briefing for **7 consecutive days** (2026-07-21 through 2026-07-26) before tonight's (2026-07-27) tick got through — `plan/DIGEST.md` sat stuck on its 2026-07-20 snapshot the entire time, and nothing surfaced this: cancelled/timed-out CI runs don't trigger the workflow's own failure-issue-filing step (`if: failure()`-gated), so no GitHub issue, comment, or AUDIT row registered any of it — the gap was only visible by reading raw `gh run list --workflow night` history by hand. Root cause, confirmed via `gh run view --log` on each run: `night.yml` shares the `march` concurrency group (`cancel-in-progress: false`, intentional — so night's commit never races a concurrent march push) but march fires roughly hourly while night fires once daily; GitHub Actions concurrency semantics only preserve the *most recently queued* run per group, so every time night's daily trigger landed mid-march-run and got queued, the *next* march trigger (usually <1h later) silently evicted the queued night run before it ever started — no error, no log, just gone. Six runs died this way (29825291068, 29914950568, 30002393933, 30088612819, 30155321672, 30199315958, all `conclusion: cancelled`); the one before that (29738523292, 07-20) got a runner slot but then blew the job's 30-minute `timeout-minutes` after a long queue wait, which *did* fire the failure step (issue #586 caught that one, but none of the six cancellations after it). Tonight's tick (run 30262709146) only succeeded because no march trigger happened to contend during its queued window — this was luck, not a fix; the starvation condition is still fully live and will keep recurring most nights unless the scheduling is decoupled. Impact: a full week with zero pulse/saga visibility, zero breadth-verdict escalation (the e2e-full row above went unremarked for a week), zero tuning-proposal cadence — the entire meta-loop's only human-facing instrument went dark with no alarm. - [ ] [user-issue #785] [MED] e2e test-isolation bug: `vote-state-pill.spec.ts` and `user-profile.spec.ts` share the same vote target (`survivor:20`) and the same authed e2e user. `user-profile.spec.ts` casts an up-vote on `survivor:20`; `vote-state-pill.spec.ts`'s "clicking up flips the pill across the three-state triad" test unconditionally clicks the up-vote button, which — because the authed user is already "up" and is the sole voter on that target — retracts the vote and drops the distinct-voter count to 0. The next test in the same file ("anon viewer renders the pair but no state pill") then asserts `/votes? so far$/` but observes "be the first to vote" instead. Deterministic under the fixed alphabetical spec order and fixed empty `seed.sql`, not flaky. Suggested fixes from the issue: (a) make the up-vote-click test branch on `data-voted` before clicking and restore state after, (b) give the anon-no-pill test its own seeded fixture target instead of relying on cross-spec execution order, or (c) stop sharing `survivor:20` across `user-profile.spec.ts` and `vote-state-pill.spec.ts` — pick distinct targets. (category: bug, source: triage, score: 4.9 — impact 7 × ease 7) — filed via triage 2026-08-22, ref #785 (blocked a prior cloud content-gap ship, see run https://github.com/daretodave/tiered/actions/runs/32542338041)

Sharon Osbourne's departure and Mel B/Heidi Klum's arrival actually happened for **Season 8** (2013), not Season 9 (2014) as both `content/shows/americas-got-talent/seasons/09-the-panel-reshapes.md` and `canon.md` currently claim — S8's real panel was still Stern/Osbourne/Mandel's *second* year together (not third), and S9's real panel was the settled Stern/Mandel/Mel B/Klum foursome's first full season, not the reshuffle year. This means the "fresh energy from a panel reshuffle" argument `canon.md` uses to rank S9 above S8 is built on the wrong season's facts — the actual reshuffle season (real S8) is currently ranked *below* the season it should be describing. Not fixed in this tick's redirect: correcting this requires rewriting both season pages' ledes/bodies plus a `canon.md` rank rationale rewrite (potentially a rank swap), which is a larger edit than the bounded, safe fix this tick's redirect scope allowed — the `watch_list` entries added to S07-S11 this tick were deliberately written to avoid asserting or contradicting either page's existing (incorrect) panel-composition claim, to not compound the error further. Fix: verify the true panel timeline against a second independent source, then rewrite S08's and S09's `lede`/body paragraphs to swap the "third consecutive season" / "panel reshapes" framing between them, and rewrite `canon.md`'s S8/S9 rationale entries to match — likely also requires re-evaluating whether the two seasons' relative canon rank (currently S9 above S8) still holds once the reshuffle-year argument is attached to the correct season. Content-only, two season files + canon.md. Spoiler discipline P0 intact (format/panel-composition facts only).
